Reading change: purification reads as becoming suited to the easy course.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000637/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1a6eae6d7e6a92c77241","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:14:4\",\"root_000637\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Arınma, daha önceki kararmış artığa dönüşmenin karşı yönlü insanî süreci olarak belirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001175/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2e6c3c4131637b70e7b9","text":"3. **Purification includes a separating cut.** `ف ل ح/B001` is cleaving or opening by cutting, while `ز ك و/B002` supplies moral purification. The response split in 87:10–13 and the choice split in 87:16–17 activate separation as a structural operation. Cleaving assigns success the role of cutting apart rival trajectories; purification names what that separation produces in the person. I infer that the nearer preference is what must be separated off. The reading makes self-purification an active discrimination, not only passive cleansing.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001175/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_36a8aa87f443774609cc","text":"87:17 supplied the retrospective test for `ف ل ح/B005`: successful purification is success because its good persists, not because it captures the nearer option criticized in 87:16. The first sheets in 87:18–19 then added a transmission surprise—the cultivated pattern is represented as inherited and repeatedly preserved.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000637/B002","root_001175/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3edcedf20901d9a01930","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:14:2\",\"root_001175\",[\"B005\"]],[\"87:14:4\",\"root_000637\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Arınma, başarının yakın kazançla değil, kalıcı iyilik üretmesiyle ölçüldüğünü gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000637/B001","root_001175/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4d8c86a4e6032cc3f700","text":"1. **Success as cultivated growth.** Lexical evidence: ف ل ح B003/B005 (tiller splitting ground; success with lasting good), ز ك و B001/B002 (growth/increase; purity/rectitude). Structural cue: after pasture and chaff, the successful one self-purifies. Assigned role: falah combines agricultural opening with lasting attainment; zakah makes growth pure rather than wasteful. Abductive move: the self is like ground that must be opened and cultivated. Reading change: success is not mere rescue; it is purified growth that lasts.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_5065244488cd775a4496","text":"1. **Success as lasting good through purification.** Lexical evidence: فلح B005 success/salvation/lasting good; زكو B002 purity/rectitude. Structural cue: the perfect \"قد أفلح\" marks achieved outcome after the negative fire scene. Assigned role: فلح names the result; تزكى names the process. Abductive move: the packet supplies success and purification; I infer that purification is the mechanism of salvation. Reading change: success is not worldly advantage but purified survival.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000637/B004","root_000637/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_5d694bcb4732938a8c3f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:14:4\",\"root_000637\",[\"B004\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Arınmak, yaratılışta verilen ölçüye ve kolaylaştırılmış yola yeniden uygun hale gelmek olarak da okunabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001175/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_67532a5e2388694ed029","text":"1. **Success is cultivated growth.** `ف ل ح/B003` is the tiller who splits ground, and `ز ك و/B001` is growth and increase. The preceding reminder in 87:9 and recollection in 87:10 provide an informational input, while the two fixed roots supply an agricultural mechanism: opening a resistant surface allows growth. I infer that reminder functions like what is admitted into cultivated ground; no seed is named. `أَفْلَحَ` consequently reads as productive cultivation culminating in self-growth, not a detached declaration of victory.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000637/B001","root_001175/B001","root_001175/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_6c73bc9fd883516188f3","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:14:2\",\"root_001175\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]],[\"87:14:4\",\"root_000637\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kurtuluş, sert toprağı yarıp büyümeye açan tarım gibi, kişinin arınarak verimli hale gelmesi biçiminde görünür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001175/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6e9dccb2b6cec73d774e","text":"3. **Preserved strength.** Lexical evidence: ف ل ح B006 (pre-dawn meal preserving strength), ب ق ي B001 in 87:17 (remaining), ح ي ي B013 (saving good). Structural cue: success is followed by remembrance/prayer and then better/enduring hereafter. Assigned role: falah is sustaining success that carries the person through. Abductive move: purification nourishes endurance. Reading change: \"has succeeded\" includes durable preservation, not just a point victory.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6f3a9e029a806f935857","text":"2. **Agricultural cleaving and growth after dried pasture.** Lexical evidence: فلح B001 cleaving/cutting and B003 tiller splitting ground; زكو B001 growth/increase; رعي B001 pasture and غثو B002 dried pasture. Structural cue: 87:4-5's vegetation cycle is reactivated in human purification. Assigned role: فلح opens the ground; زكو makes it grow cleanly. Abductive move: the packet supplies farming and growth branches; I infer a cultivated-self mechanism. Reading change: \"prospered\" becomes \"was tilled into purified growth.\"","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_7342175bdbd3e4958b46","text":"3. **Purification as answer to dark refuse.** Lexical evidence: زكو B002 purity; حوي B006 dark coloring and غثو B004 unregarded refuse in 87:5. Structural cue: purification follows the image of darkened residue and the fire-suspension. Assigned role: تزكى is the counter-transformation. Abductive move: the packet supplies dark refuse and purity; I infer a reversal of degradation. Reading change: the ayah is not merely a moral maxim; it is the way not to become waste.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001175/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8a9aba8f1f455fbe7ea3","text":"2. **Purification defines the kind of success that lasts.** `ف ل ح/B005` gives success with lasting good, and `ز ك و/B002` gives purification and rectitude. The later correction in 87:16–17 contrasts preference for near life with what is `خَيْرٌ وَأَبْقَىٰ`. Purification supplies the process, and durable good supplies the criterion of success. I infer that purification causes or discloses that durability.
